 *    NO & ASSETS kosong supaya user isi/pilih manual.
 */
function getMachineExchangeAutofill_(data) {
  var ticket = String((data && data.ticket) || '').trim();
  var locationName = String((data && data.locationName) || '').trim();

  var result = {
    success: true,
    vmTypeOnSite: '',
    vmc: { found: false, noVMC: '', assets: '', serialNumber: '', machineDetail: '', sheetName: '' }
  };

  if (ticket) {
    try {
      var ssJbdtbk = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_JABODETABEK);
      var sheetJbdtbk = ssJbdtbk.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME_JABODETABEK);
      if (sheetJbdtbk) {
        var rowNum = _findJabodetabekRowByTicket_(sheetJbdtbk, ticket);
        if (rowNum) {
          var headersJbdtbk = sheetJbdtbk
            .getRange(HEADER_ROW_JABODETABEK, 1, 1, LAST_COL_JABODETABEK)
            .getValues()[0]
            .map(function (h) { return h === '' || h === null ? '' : String(h).trim().toLowerCase(); });
          var vmTypeIdx = headersJbdtbk.indexOf('vm type on site');
          var vmTypeCol = vmTypeIdx === -1 ? 11 : (vmTypeIdx + 1); // fallback kolom K (11)
          result.vmTypeOnSite = String(sheetJbdtbk.getRange(rowNum, vmTypeCol).getValue() || '').trim();
        }
      }
    } catch (errVmType) {
      // abaikan, biarkan vmTypeOnSite kosong — frontend fallback ke nilai
      // "VM TYPE ON SITE" yang sudah ada di card (dari sheet "Plan Task").
    }
  }

  if (locationName) {
    var target = _findLatestStockOpnameRowByLocationAcrossSheets_(locationName);
    if (target) {
      result.vmc = {
        found: true,
        noVMC: target.noVMC,
        assets: target.assets,
        serialNumber: target.serialNumber,
        machineDetail: target.machineDetail,
        sheetName: target.sheetName
      };
    }
  }

  return result;
}

/**
 * Tentukan sheet tujuan (di antara KELIMA STOCK_OPNAME_ALLOWED_SHEETS) untuk
 * baris baru "Machine Exchange". Prioritas:
 *   1. hintSheetName (dikirim frontend dari hasil getMachineExchangeAutofill_
 *      saat popup dibuka) kalau valid.
 *   2. Kalau tidak ada/tidak valid (mis. VMC NO diisi manual karena auto-fill
 *      tidak ketemu): cari ulang lewat VMC NO itu sendiri di 5 sheet
 *      (_findLatestStockOpnameRowByVmcAcrossSheets_) — barangkali VMC NO itu
 *      sudah tercatat di salah satu sheet.
 *   3. Kalau masih tidak ketemu: tebak sheet dari kecocokan teks MACHINE
 *      TYPE (kolom E) — sheet dengan jumlah baris MACHINE TYPE yang cocok
 *      TERBANYAK yang dipakai.
 *   4. Fallback terakhir: sheet pertama di STOCK_OPNAME_ALLOWED_SHEETS
 *      ("Mart-Unique Machine Report").
 */
function _resolveMachineExchangeTargetSheet_(hintSheetName, vmcNo, machineType) {
  if (hintSheetName && STOCK_OPNAME_ALLOWED_SHEETS.indexOf(hintSheetName) !== -1) {
    return hintSheetName;
  }

  var byVmc = _findLatestStockOpnameRowByVmcAcrossSheets_(vmcNo);
  if (byVmc) return byVmc.sheetName;

  var typeNorm = _normKeyForLookup_(machineType);
  if (typeNorm) {
    var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_INVENTORY_JS);
    var bestSheet = null;
    var bestCount = 0;
    STOCK_OPNAME_ALLOWED_SHEETS.forEach(function (sheetName) {
      var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName);
      if (!sheet) return;
      var lastRow = _stockOpnameLastDataRow_(sheet);
      var numRows = lastRow - STOCK_OPNAME_DATA_START_ROW + 1;
      if (numRows <= 0) return;
      var values = sheet.getRange(STOCK_OPNAME_DATA_START_ROW, STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.machineType, numRows, 1).getValues();
      var count = 0;
      values.forEach(function (row) {
        if (_normKeyForLookup_(row[0]) === typeNorm) count++;
      });
      if (count > bestCount) {
        bestCount = count;
        bestSheet = sheetName;
      }
    });
    if (bestSheet) return bestSheet;
  }

  return STOCK_OPNAME_ALLOWED_SHEETS[0];
}

/**
 * Catat isian section "Machine Exchange (Sent To WH)" (data.machineExchange,
 * dikirim dari submitDispatchDelivered di index.html) sebagai BARIS BARU ke
 * salah satu dari 5 sheet Stock Opname — TIDAK PERNAH menimpa baris yang
 * sudah berisi data (sama seperti saveStockOpname_ / Stock Opname manual,
 * pakai _stockOpnameLastDataRow_ untuk cari baris kosong berikutnya).
 *
 * Kolom yang ditulis:
 *   A = VMC NO        (data.machineExchange.vmcNo — auto atau isian manual)
 *   B = SERIAL NUMBER  (dari baris Inventory yang ketemu lewat lookup, kalau
 *                       ada — kosong kalau VMC NO manual & belum tercatat)
 *   C = EX. LOCATION   (Location Name Cardview, data.locationName)
 *   D = MACHINE DETAIL (sama seperti B)
 *   E = MACHINE TYPE   (VM TYPE ON SITE dari Cardview/popup)
 *   H = INBOUND DATE   (Loading Date (Actual), format mm/dd/yyyy)
 *   K = FINAL STATUS   (dipilih user di popup)
 *   L = ASSETS         (auto atau dipilih user di popup)
 * Kolom F, G, I, J, M sengaja dibiarkan KOSONG. Dropdown (data validation)
 * kolom E/J/K/L/M diwarisi dari baris di atasnya, sama seperti
 * saveStockOpname_, supaya baris baru tetap ikut format cell yang sudah ada.
 *
 * Mengembalikan { found: false, skipped: true } (BUKAN error) kalau VMC NO
 * kosong, supaya penyimpanan Delivered ke Jabodetabek (yang sudah berhasil)
 * tidak ikut gagal.
 */
function _recordMachineExchangeInventory_(data) {
  var me = (data && data.machineExchange) || {};
  var vmcNo = String(me.vmcNo || '').trim();
  var vmTypeOnSite = String(me.vmTypeOnSite || '').trim();
  var finalStatus = String(me.finalStatus || '').trim();
  var assets = String(me.assets || '').trim();
  var serialNumber = String(me.serialNumber || '').trim();
  var machineDetail = String(me.machineDetail || '').trim();
  var locationName = String(data.locationName || '').trim();
  var loadingDateActual = data.loadingDateActual;

  if (!vmcNo) {
    return { found: false, skipped: true, message: 'VMC NO Machine Exchange kosong, tidak dicatat ke Inventory JS.' };
  }

  // ANTI-DOUBLE: kalau baris Machine Exchange yang isinya sama persis (VMC NO +
  // EX. LOCATION + INBOUND DATE + FINAL STATUS + ASSETS) SUDAH ada di salah
  // satu dari 5 sheet Stock Opname, JANGAN tambah baris lagi. Lihat blok
  // komentar ANTI-DOUBLE di dekat _stockOpnameDateSerial_.
  var dupMe = _findDuplicateStockOpnameRow_({
    vmcNo: vmcNo,
    exLocation: locationName,
    inboundSerial: _stockOpnameDateSerial_(loadingDateActual),
    finalStatus: finalStatus,
    assets: assets
  });
  if (dupMe) {
    return {
      found: true,
      inserted: false,
      duplicateSkipped: true,
      sheetName: dupMe.sheetName,
      rowNumber: dupMe.rowNumber,
      message: 'Baris Machine Exchange yang sama sudah tercatat di Inventory JS — tidak ditambahkan lagi supaya tidak double.'
    };
  }

  var targetSheetName = _resolveMachineExchangeTargetSheet_(me.sheetName, vmcNo, vmTypeOnSite);

  // Kalau SERIAL NUMBER/MACHINE DETAIL belum terisi (mis. VMC NO diisi manual,
  // bukan hasil auto-fill lookup lokasi), tapi VMC NO itu ternyata SUDAH
  // tercatat di salah satu dari 5 sheet, ambil dari baris yang sudah ada itu
  // supaya tidak kosong.
  if (!serialNumber || !machineDetail) {
    var existing = _findLatestStockOpnameRowByVmcAcrossSheets_(vmcNo);
    if (existing) {
      var ssLookup = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_INVENTORY_JS);
      var sheetLookup = ssLookup.getSheetByName(existing.sheetName);
      if (sheetLookup) {
        var existingRow = sheetLookup.getRange(existing.rowNumber, 1, 1, 13).getValues()[0];
        if (!serialNumber) serialNumber = String(existingRow[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.serialNumber - 1] || '').trim();
        if (!machineDetail) machineDetail = String(existingRow[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.machineDetail - 1] || '').trim();
      }
    }
  }

  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_INVENTORY_JS);
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(targetSheetName);
  if (!sheet) {
    return { found: false, error: 'Sheet "' + targetSheetName + '" tidak ditemukan.' };
  }

  var lastRow = _stockOpnameLastDataRow_(sheet);
  var targetRow = Math.max(lastRow + 1, STOCK_OPNAME_DATA_START_ROW);

  var lastCol = 13; // A - M
  var rowValues = new Array(lastCol).fill('');
  rowValues[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.noVMC - 1] = vmcNo;
  rowValues[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.serialNumber - 1] = serialNumber;
  rowValues[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.exLocation - 1] = locationName;
  rowValues[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.machineDetail - 1] = machineDetail;
  rowValues[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.machineType - 1] = vmTypeOnSite;
  rowValues[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.inboundDate - 1] = _stockOpnameDateSerial_(loadingDateActual); // FIX tanggal mundur sehari: serial-date (bebas timezone)
  rowValues[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.finalStatus - 1] = finalStatus;
  rowValues[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.assets - 1] = assets;

  sheet.getRange(targetRow, 1, 1, lastCol).setValues([rowValues]);
  sheet.getRange(targetRow, STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.inboundDate, 1, 1).setNumberFormat('mm/dd/yyyy');

  var templateRow = targetRow > STOCK_OPNAME_DATA_START_ROW ? targetRow - 1 : STOCK_OPNAME_DATA_START_ROW;
  STOCK_OPNAME_DROPDOWN_COLS.forEach(function (col) {
    var srcCell = sheet.getRange(templateRow, col);
    var rule = srcCell.getDataValidation();
    if (rule) {
      sheet.getRange(targetRow, col).setDataValidation(rule);
    }
  });

  return { found: true, sheetName: targetSheetName, rowNumber: targetRow };
}

/**
 * Tentukan baris tujuan penulisan balik hasil Popup Pengerjaan Task di sheet
 * asal. Prioritas: rowNumber hasil "getTeamPrepareTaskDetail_" (hintRowNumber,
 * dikirim balik dari frontend sebagai "rowNumberDetail") kalau baris itu MASIH
 * cocok VMC NO + Ex. Location saat ini (antisipasi baris bergeser karena ada
 * yang insert/delete baris di antara buka Popup dan klik Simpan). Kalau tidak
 * cocok lagi (atau hint tidak dikirim), cari ulang lewat VMC NO + Ex. Location.
 */
function _resolveTeamPrepareSourceRow_(sheet, hintRowNumber, noVMC, exLocation) {
  var noVMCNorm = String(noVMC || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  var exLocNorm = String(exLocation || '').trim().toLowerCase();

  if (hintRowNumber && hintRowNumber >= STOCK_OPNAME_DATA_START_ROW && hintRowNumber <= sheet.getLastRow()) {
    var check = sheet.getRange(hintRowNumber, 1, 1, 13).getValues()[0];
    var checkVmc = String(check[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.noVMC - 1] || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    var checkLoc = String(check[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.exLocation - 1] || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (checkVmc === noVMCNorm && (!exLocNorm || checkLoc === exLocNorm)) {
      return hintRowNumber;
    }
  }
  return _findStockOpnameRowByVmcAndLocation_(sheet, noVMC, exLocation);
}

/**
 * ============================================================
 *  TASK TEAM PREPARE (menu "Team Prepare" -> "Task Team Prepare")
 *  Cardview dari sheet "Task Machine Preparation" (SHEET_NAME_TASK_MACHINE_PREP,
 *  spreadsheet SPREADSHEET_ID_DISPATCH_V2) — hanya baris STATUS "Open" (kosong
 *  dianggap "Open" juga, untuk baris lama sebelum kolom STATUS ada) yang
 *  ditampilkan. Tombol "Start" pada tiap card membuka Popup pengerjaan Task
 *  yang menampilkan detail lengkap mesin tsb, diambil langsung dari sheet asal
 *  (SHEET ASAL, salah satu dari MACHINE_PREP_SHEETS) pada spreadsheet
 *  "JABODETABEK - JS Coffee & Mart Machine Inventory Report"
 *  (SPREADSHEET_ID_INVENTORY_JS) — dicocokkan lewat VMC NO (+ Serial Number).
 *  Tombol "Cancel" pada card langsung menandai STATUS baris tsb "Cancelled"
 *  (tanpa membuka Popup). Hasil isian Popup ("Simpan") dicatat ke sheet baru
 *  "Task Team Prepare" pada spreadsheet SPREADSHEET_ID_DISPATCH_V2, DAN
 *  menandai baris sumbernya di "Task Machine Preparation" jadi STATUS "Done"
 *  supaya card-nya hilang dari cardview.
 * ============================================================
 */
// Kolom tujuan tetap (fixed) untuk FINAL STATUS: K = 11.
var TEAM_PREPARE_FINAL_STATUS_COL_TARGET = STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.finalStatus; // K (11)

var SHEET_NAME_TEAM_PREPARE_RESULT = 'Task Team Prepare';
// Kolom A-N seperti semula + 'FOTO BUKTI' ditambahkan sebagai kolom ke-15
// (jatuh persis di kolom O) — diisi dari Foto Bukti Selesai yang diunggah
// lewat Popup "Pengerjaan Task" (menu Task Team Prepare), lihat
// uploadTeamPreparePhoto_ / _uploadTeamPreparePhotosToDrive_ di bawah.
// _getOrCreateTeamPrepareResultSheet_ SELALU menulis ulang baris header
// (baris 1) sesuai array ini setiap dipanggil, jadi kalau sheet lama belum
// punya header "FOTO BUKTI" di kolom O, otomatis ditambahkan begitu Popup
// berikutnya disimpan (tidak perlu setup manual).
var TEAM_PREPARE_RESULT_HEADERS = [
  'Timestamp', 'VMC NO', 'SERIAL NUMBER', 'MACHINE TYPE', 'EX. LOCATION',
  'MACHINE DETAIL', 'INBOUND DATE', 'ASSETS', 'ALOKASI', 'FINAL STATUS',
  'KETERANGAN', 'PIC', 'SHEET ASAL', 'DETAIL CHECKLIST (JSON)', 'FOTO BUKTI'
];

// Pilihan dropdown tetap untuk semua item checklist A.x (SPAREPART, LCD &
// Touchscreen, Coffee Maker, dst) di ketiga jenis sheet sumber di bawah.
var TEAM_PREPARE_DROPDOWN_OPTIONS = ['OK', 'NOT OK', 'AVAIL', 'NOT AVAIL'];

/**
 * Ubah huruf kolom (A, B, ..., Z, AA, AB, ...) jadi nomor kolom 1-based.
 */
function _colLetterToNum_(letters) {
  var col = 0;
  var s = String(letters).toUpperCase();
  for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) {
    col = col * 26 + (s.charCodeAt(i) - 64);
  }
  return col;
}

/**
 * Definisi checklist tambahan per sheet sumber (persis sesuai spesifikasi):
 *  Semua label ada di baris header 5 sheet Stock Opname = STOCK_OPNAME_HEADER_ROW (baris 3):
 *  - Mart-Unique Machine Report : 2 blok (Sparepart + Pre-RTD Check)
 *  - TT Machine Report          : 1 blok (Sparepart, S3:BQ3 — 51 kolom)
 *  - FS ICE Machine Report      : 6 blok (LCD s.d. Ice Maker)
 * inputType 'chip'      -> toggle slide (ON = "DONE", OFF = "")
 * inputType 'dropdown'  -> pilihan TEAM_PREPARE_DROPDOWN_OPTIONS
 * Sheet yang tidak terdaftar di sini (mis. "FS HOT Machine Report") tetap
 * bisa dibuka Popup-nya, hanya saja tanpa blok checklist tambahan (cuma
 * field dasar A-M).
 */
var TEAM_PREPARE_CHECKLIST_DEFS = {
  'Mart-Unique Machine Report': [
    {
      key: 'sparepart', title: 'A.2 SPAREPART', headerRow: STOCK_OPNAME_HEADER_ROW,
      startCol: _colLetterToNum_('U'), endCol: _colLetterToNum_('AH'), inputType: 'dropdown'
    },
    {
      key: 'preRtdCheck', title: 'A.1 PRE-RTD CHECK', headerRow: STOCK_OPNAME_HEADER_ROW,
      startCol: _colLetterToNum_('AI'), endCol: _colLetterToNum_('AM'), inputType: 'chip'
    }
  ],
  'TT Machine Report': [
    {
      key: 'sparepart', title: 'A.1 SPAREPART', headerRow: STOCK_OPNAME_HEADER_ROW,
      startCol: _colLetterToNum_('S'), endCol: _colLetterToNum_('BQ'), inputType: 'dropdown'
    }
  ],
  'FS ICE Machine Report': [
    {
      key: 'lcdTouchscreen', title: 'A.1 LCD & Touchscreen', headerRow: STOCK_OPNAME_HEADER_ROW,
      startCol: _colLetterToNum_('S'), endCol: _colLetterToNum_('Z'), inputType: 'dropdown'
    },
    {
      key: 'coffeeMaker', title: 'A.2 Coffee Maker', headerRow: STOCK_OPNAME_HEADER_ROW,
      startCol: _colLetterToNum_('AA'), endCol: _colLetterToNum_('AE'), inputType: 'dropdown'
    },
    {
      key: 'boilerCoffeeMaker', title: 'A.3 Boiler Coffee Marker', headerRow: STOCK_OPNAME_HEADER_ROW,
      startCol: _colLetterToNum_('AF'), endCol: _colLetterToNum_('AJ'), inputType: 'dropdown'
    },
    {
      key: 'cupDispenseCoffeeMaker', title: 'A.4 Cup Dispense Coffee Maker', headerRow: STOCK_OPNAME_HEADER_ROW,
      startCol: _colLetterToNum_('AK'), endCol: _colLetterToNum_('AQ'), inputType: 'dropdown'
    },
    {
      key: 'mechanicalCoffeeMaker', title: 'A.5 Mechanical Coffee Maker', headerRow: STOCK_OPNAME_HEADER_ROW,
      startCol: _colLetterToNum_('AR'), endCol: _colLetterToNum_('BB'), inputType: 'dropdown'
    },
    {
      key: 'iceMaker', title: 'A. 6 Ice Maker', headerRow: STOCK_OPNAME_HEADER_ROW,
      startCol: _colLetterToNum_('BC'), endCol: _colLetterToNum_('BP'), inputType: 'dropdown'
    }
  ]
};

/**
 * Ambil seluruh Task dari sheet "Task Machine Preparation" yang STATUS-nya
 * masih "Open" (termasuk baris lama yang kolom STATUS-nya masih kosong),
 * untuk ditampilkan sebagai Cardview di menu "Task Team Prepare". Setiap
 * baris hasil disertai "rowNumber" supaya bisa dipakai untuk tombol
 * Start/Cancel (action 'updateTaskMachinePrepStatus') dan supaya baris
 * sumbernya bisa ditandai "Done" begitu Popup pengerjaan disimpan.
 */
function getTeamPrepareCards_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_DISPATCH_V2);
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME_TASK_MACHINE_PREP);
  if (!sheet) {
    return { success: true, data: [] };
  }

  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) {
    return { success: true, data: [] };
  }

  var numRows = lastRow - 1;
  var values = sheet.getRange(2, 1, numRows, TASK_MACHINE_PREP_HEADERS.length).getValues();

  var data = [];
  values.forEach(function (row, idx) {
    var hasData = row.some(function (cell) { return cell !== '' && cell !== null; });
    if (!hasData) return;

    var status = String(row[TASK_MACHINE_PREP_STATUS_COL - 1] || '').trim() || 'Open';
    if (status !== 'Open') return;

    var timestampRaw = row[0];
    data.push({
      rowNumber: idx + 2,
      timestamp: (timestampRaw instanceof Date)
        ? Utilities.formatDate(timestampRaw, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm')
        : (timestampRaw || ''),
      noVMC: row[1] || '',
      serialNumber: row[2] || '',
      exLocation: row[3] || '',
      machineType: row[4] || '',
      warehouse: row[5] || '',
      assets: row[6] || '',
      alokasi: row[7] || '',
      sheet: row[8] || ''
    });
  });

  return { success: true, data: data };
}

/**
 * Tandai STATUS satu baris di sheet "Task Machine Preparation" (dipakai
 * tombol "Cancel" di Cardview Team Prepare, dan otomatis oleh
 * submitTeamPrepareTask_ untuk menandai "Done" begitu Popup disimpan).
 */
function updateTaskMachinePrepStatus_(data) {
  var rowNumber = Number(data.rowNumber);
  var status = String(data.status || '').trim();

  if (!rowNumber || rowNumber < 2) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Nomor baris tidak valid.' };
  }
  if (TASK_MACHINE_PREP_ALLOWED_STATUSES.indexOf(status) === -1) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Status tidak valid: ' + status };
  }

  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_DISPATCH_V2);
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME_TASK_MACHINE_PREP);
  if (!sheet) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Sheet "' + SHEET_NAME_TASK_MACHINE_PREP + '" tidak ditemukan.' };
  }
  if (rowNumber > sheet.getLastRow()) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Baris tidak ditemukan di sheet "' + SHEET_NAME_TASK_MACHINE_PREP + '".' };
  }

  sheet.getRange(rowNumber, TASK_MACHINE_PREP_STATUS_COL).setValue(status);
  return { success: true, message: 'Status berhasil diperbarui menjadi ' + status + '.', rowNumber: rowNumber, status: status };
}

/**
 * Simpan nilai PIC (kolom K, TASK_MACHINE_PREP_PIC_COL) satu baris di sheet
 * "Task Machine Preparation" — dipanggil saat Popup Pengerjaan Task disimpan
 * (menu "Task Team Prepare"). Pakai _getOrCreateTaskMachinePrepSheet_ (bukan
 * getSheetByName langsung) supaya header selalu disegarkan sesuai
 * TASK_MACHINE_PREP_HEADERS terbaru (termasuk kolom "PIC" kalau baris header
 * lama belum punya kolom itu).
 */
function _setTaskMachinePrepPic_(ss, rowNumber, pic) {
  if (!rowNumber || rowNumber < 2) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Nomor baris Task Machine Preparation tidak valid.' };
  }
  var sheet = _getOrCreateTaskMachinePrepSheet_(ss);
  if (rowNumber > sheet.getLastRow()) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Baris tidak ditemukan di sheet "' + SHEET_NAME_TASK_MACHINE_PREP + '".' };
  }
  sheet.getRange(rowNumber, TASK_MACHINE_PREP_PIC_COL).setValue(pic || '');
  return { success: true };
}

/**
 * Ubah nilai kolom Inbound Date (bisa objek Date, string, atau kosong) jadi
 * milidetik epoch supaya bisa dibandingkan. Nilai tidak valid/kosong ->
 * -Infinity (supaya baris tanpa Inbound Date selalu kalah dibanding baris
 * yang punya tanggal, saat dipakai sebagai kriteria "paling baru").
 */
function _teamPrepareDateMs_(val) {
  if (val instanceof Date) {
    return isNaN(val.getTime()) ? -Infinity : val.getTime();
  }
  if (val !== '' && val !== null && val !== undefined) {
    var parsed = new Date(val);
    if (!isNaN(parsed.getTime())) return parsed.getTime();
  }
  return -Infinity;
}

/**
 * Cari nomor baris (1-based, sheet asli) di salah satu sheet Stock Opname
 * (Mart-Unique/TT Machine/FS ICE/FS HOT) yang kolom A (No VMC) cocok, dan
 * kalau serialNumber diisi ikut dipakai sebagai pencocokan tambahan supaya
 * lebih presisi kalau ada No VMC yang kebetulan sama (kalau ada baris yang
 * cocok Serial Number-nya, hanya baris-baris itu yang dipertimbangkan;
 * kalau tidak ada, semua baris dengan No VMC yang sama tetap dipertimbangkan).
 * Kalau ketemu lebih dari satu baris cocok (mis. mesin yang sama pernah
 * dicatat beberapa kali), yang dipilih adalah baris dengan kolom INBOUND
 * DATE (kolom H) PALING BARU. Pencarian No VMC/Serial Number case-
 * insensitive & trim. Return -1 kalau tidak ketemu sama sekali.
 */
function _findStockOpnameRowNumber_(sheet, noVMC, serialNumber) {
  var noVMCNorm = String(noVMC || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!noVMCNorm) return -1;
  var serialNorm = String(serialNumber || '').trim().toLowerCase();

  var lastRow = _stockOpnameLastDataRow_(sheet);
  var numRows = lastRow - STOCK_OPNAME_DATA_START_ROW + 1;
  if (numRows <= 0) return -1;

  var values = sheet.getRange(STOCK_OPNAME_DATA_START_ROW, 1, numRows, 13).getValues();

  var candidatesNoVMCOnly = [];
  var candidatesWithSerial = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < values.length; i++) {
    var rowNoVMC = String(values[i][STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.noVMC - 1] || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (rowNoVMC !== noVMCNorm) continue;

    var rowNumber = STOCK_OPNAME_DATA_START_ROW + i;
    var inboundMs = _teamPrepareDateMs_(values[i][STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.inboundDate - 1]);
    candidatesNoVMCOnly.push({ rowNumber: rowNumber, inboundMs: inboundMs });

    var rowSerial = String(values[i][STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.serialNumber - 1] || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (serialNorm && rowSerial === serialNorm) {
      candidatesWithSerial.push({ rowNumber: rowNumber, inboundMs: inboundMs });
    }
  }

  // Prioritaskan baris yang Serial Number-nya juga cocok (kalau ada), supaya
  // tidak salah ambil mesin lain yang kebetulan No VMC-nya sama.
  var candidates = (serialNorm && candidatesWithSerial.length > 0) ? candidatesWithSerial : candidatesNoVMCOnly;
  if (!candidates.length) return -1;

  // Pilih Inbound Date paling baru. Kalau lebih dari satu baris punya
  // Inbound Date yang sama (termasuk sama-sama kosong), baris paling bawah
  // (rowNumber terbesar) yang dipakai, karena itu biasanya entri terbaru.
  var best = candidates[0];
  for (var j = 1; j < candidates.length; j++) {
    var c = candidates[j];
    if (c.inboundMs > best.inboundMs || (c.inboundMs === best.inboundMs && c.rowNumber > best.rowNumber)) {
      best = c;
    }
  }
  return best.rowNumber;
}

/**
 * Ambil detail lengkap satu mesin untuk Popup pengerjaan Task (menu "Task
 * Team Prepare", tombol "Start"). Data dasar (Machine Detail, Inbound Date,
 * Assets, Alokasi, dst — kolom A-M) diambil dari sheet asal (params.sheetAsal,
 * harus salah satu STOCK_OPNAME_ALLOWED_SHEETS) pada spreadsheet
 * SPREADSHEET_ID_INVENTORY_JS ("JABODETABEK - JS Coffee & Mart Machine
 * Inventory Report"), dicocokkan lewat VMC NO (+ Serial Number kalau ada).
 * Kalau sheet asal terdaftar di TEAM_PREPARE_CHECKLIST_DEFS, blok checklist
 * tambahan (SPAREPART / PRE-RTD CHECK / LCD & Touchscreen / dst) ikut
 * disertakan, label diambil dari baris header masing-masing definisi &
 * nilai saat ini diambil dari baris data mesin yang cocok.
 */
function getTeamPrepareTaskDetail_(params) {
  var sheetAsal = String((params && params.sheetAsal) || '').trim();
  var noVMC = String((params && params.noVMC) || '').trim();
  var serialNumber = String((params && params.serialNumber) || '').trim();

  if (!sheetAsal || STOCK_OPNAME_ALLOWED_SHEETS.indexOf(sheetAsal) === -1) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Sheet asal tidak valid: ' + sheetAsal };
  }
  if (!noVMC) {
    return { success: false, message: 'VMC NO wajib diisi.' };
  }

  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_INVENTORY_JS);
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(sheetAsal);
  if (!sheet) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Sheet "' + sheetAsal + '" tidak ditemukan di spreadsheet Inventory.' };
  }

  var rowNumber = _findStockOpnameRowNumber_(sheet, noVMC, serialNumber);
  if (rowNumber === -1) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Data mesin "' + noVMC + '" tidak ditemukan di sheet "' + sheetAsal + '".' };
  }

  var baseValues = sheet.getRange(rowNumber, 1, 1, 13).getValues()[0];
  var inboundDateRaw = baseValues[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.inboundDate - 1];
  var inboundDate = (inboundDateRaw instanceof Date)
    ? Utilities.formatDate(inboundDateRaw, ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd')
    : (inboundDateRaw || '');

  var base = {
    noVMC: baseValues[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.noVMC - 1] || '',
    serialNumber: baseValues[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.serialNumber - 1] || '',
    exLocation: baseValues[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.exLocation - 1] || '',
    machineDetail: baseValues[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.machineDetail - 1] || '',
    machineType: baseValues[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.machineType - 1] || '',
    inboundDate: inboundDate,
    assets: baseValues[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.assets - 1] || '',
    alokasi: baseValues[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.alokasi - 1] || '',
    finalStatus: baseValues[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.finalStatus - 1] || ''
  };

  var defs = TEAM_PREPARE_CHECKLIST_DEFS[sheetAsal] || [];
  var checklist = defs.map(function (def) {
    var numCols = def.endCol - def.startCol + 1;
    var labels = sheet.getRange(def.headerRow, def.startCol, 1, numCols).getValues()[0];
    var values = sheet.getRange(rowNumber, def.startCol, 1, numCols).getValues()[0];

    var items = [];
    for (var c = 0; c < numCols; c++) {
      var label = String(labels[c] || '').trim();
      if (!label) continue; // lewati kolom tanpa label header
      items.push({
        col: def.startCol + c,
        label: label,
        value: values[c] === null ? '' : values[c]
      });
    }

    return {
      key: def.key,
      title: def.title,
      inputType: def.inputType,
      options: def.inputType === 'dropdown' ? TEAM_PREPARE_DROPDOWN_OPTIONS : null,
      items: items
    };
  });

  return { success: true, rowNumber: rowNumber, base: base, checklist: checklist };
}

/**
 * Teks header KETERANGAN tidak selalu persis "KETERANGAN" di tiap sheet
 * sumber — override di sini kalau beda. Sheet yang tidak terdaftar di sini
 * pakai default 'KETERANGAN'.
 */
var TEAM_PREPARE_KETERANGAN_HEADER_TEXT = {
  'Mart-Unique Machine Report': 'KETERANGAN/SERIAL NUMBER', // kolom AR
  'FS HOT Machine Report': 'Keterangan'
};

/**
 * Tentukan baris header yang dipakai untuk mencari kolom "KETERANGAN" di
 * sheet asal — ikut baris header yang sama dengan blok checklist tambahan
 * sheet itu (TEAM_PREPARE_CHECKLIST_DEFS[sheetAsal][0].headerRow, sekarang
 * semuanya = STOCK_OPNAME_HEADER_ROW / baris 3). Untuk sheet yang belum terdaftar checklist
 * tambahannya (mis. "FS HOT Machine Report"), fallback ke STOCK_OPNAME_HEADER_ROW.
 */
function _teamPrepareHeaderRowForSheet_(sheetAsal) {
  var defs = TEAM_PREPARE_CHECKLIST_DEFS[sheetAsal];
  if (defs && defs.length && defs[0].headerRow) return defs[0].headerRow;
  return STOCK_OPNAME_HEADER_ROW;
}

/**
 * Cari nomor kolom (1-based) di sheet yang header-nya (baris headerRow)
 * cocok persis dengan headerText (trim + case-insensitive). Return -1 kalau
 * tidak ketemu. Dipakai supaya kolom KETERANGAN mengikuti header yang
 * benar-benar ada di masing-masing sheet, bukan posisi kolom tetap — karena
 * posisinya bisa beda-beda per sheet.
 */
function _findColumnByHeaderText_(sheet, headerRow, headerText) {
  var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn();
  if (lastCol < 1) return -1;
  var headers = sheet.getRange(headerRow, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0];
  var target = String(headerText || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  for (var c = 0; c < headers.length; c++) {
    if (String(headers[c] || '').trim().toUpperCase() === target) {
      return c + 1;
    }
  }
  return -1;
}

/**
 * Tulis balik hasil Popup Pengerjaan Task ke baris asalnya di spreadsheet
 * "JABODETABEK - JS Coffee & Mart Machine Inventory Report"
 * (SPREADSHEET_ID_INVENTORY_JS), sheet sesuai data.sheetAsal, baris dicari
 * lewat VMC NO + EX. LOCATION (lihat _resolveTeamPrepareSourceRow_):
 *  - FINAL STATUS  -> kolom K (TEAM_PREPARE_FINAL_STATUS_COL_TARGET, tetap).
 *    Nilai ditulis persis sesuai teks pilihan dropdown yang ada di kolom itu
 *    — warna ikut otomatis karena kolom ini sudah pakai Conditional
 *    Formatting berbasis nilai (bukan pewarnaan manual per sel), jadi tidak
 *    perlu setBackground di sini.
 *  - KETERANGAN    -> kolom dicari lewat teks header sesuai
 *    TEAM_PREPARE_KETERANGAN_HEADER_TEXT (default "KETERANGAN", override per
 *    sheet — mis. "KETERANGAN/SERIAL NUMBER" utk Mart-Unique, "Keterangan"
 *    utk FS HOT) pada baris header sheet tsb (_findColumnByHeaderText_ +
 *    _teamPrepareHeaderRowForSheet_), BUKAN posisi kolom tetap.
 *  - Checklist tambahan (SPAREPART / PRE-RTD CHECK / LCD & Touchscreen /
 *    Coffee Maker / Boiler Coffee Marker / Cup Dispense Coffee Maker /
 *    Mechanical Coffee Maker / Ice Maker / dst) -> ditulis ke kolom asalnya
 *    masing-masing lewat item.col (posisi kolom ini dikirim balik oleh
 *    frontend persis seperti yang diterima dari getTeamPrepareTaskDetail_,
 *    yang sumbernya TEAM_PREPARE_CHECKLIST_DEFS per sheetAsal).
 */
function _writeTeamPrepareBackToSource_(data) {
  var sheetAsal = String(data.sheetAsal || '').trim();
  if (!sheetAsal || STOCK_OPNAME_ALLOWED_SHEETS.indexOf(sheetAsal) === -1) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Sheet asal tidak valid untuk disimpan balik: ' + sheetAsal };
  }

  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_INVENTORY_JS);
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(sheetAsal);
  if (!sheet) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Sheet "' + sheetAsal + '" tidak ditemukan di spreadsheet Inventory.' };
  }

  var rowNumber = _resolveTeamPrepareSourceRow_(sheet, Number(data.rowNumberDetail), data.noVMC, data.exLocation);
  if (rowNumber === -1) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: 'Baris sumber untuk VMC NO "' + data.noVMC + '" / EX. LOCATION "' + (data.exLocation || '-') +
        '" tidak ditemukan di sheet "' + sheetAsal + '".'
    };
  }

  if (data.finalStatus) {
    sheet.getRange(rowNumber, TEAM_PREPARE_FINAL_STATUS_COL_TARGET).setValue(data.finalStatus);
  }

  var keteranganHeaderRow = _teamPrepareHeaderRowForSheet_(sheetAsal);
  var keteranganHeaderText = TEAM_PREPARE_KETERANGAN_HEADER_TEXT[sheetAsal] || 'KETERANGAN';
  var keteranganCol = _findColumnByHeaderText_(sheet, keteranganHeaderRow, keteranganHeaderText);
  if (keteranganCol === -1) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: 'Kolom header "' + keteranganHeaderText + '" tidak ditemukan di sheet "' + sheetAsal +
        '" (baris header ' + keteranganHeaderRow + '). Pastikan header tsb sudah ada di sheet tsb.'
    };
  }
  sheet.getRange(rowNumber, keteranganCol).setValue(data.keterangan || '');

  var checklist = data.checklist || [];
  checklist.forEach(function (block) {
    (block.items || []).forEach(function (item) {
      var col = Number(item.col);
      if (!col) return;
      sheet.getRange(rowNumber, col).setValue(item.value || '');
    });
  });

  return { success: true, rowNumber: rowNumber, sheetAsal: sheetAsal };
}

function _getOrCreateTeamPrepareResultSheet_(ss) {
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME_TEAM_PREPARE_RESULT);
  if (!sheet) {
    sheet = ss.insertSheet(SHEET_NAME_TEAM_PREPARE_RESULT);
  }
  sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, TEAM_PREPARE_RESULT_HEADERS.length).setValues([TEAM_PREPARE_RESULT_HEADERS]);
  return sheet;
}

/**
 * Simpan hasil Popup pengerjaan Task (menu "Task Team Prepare", tombol
 * "Simpan") ke sheet baru "Task Team Prepare" pada spreadsheet
 * SPREADSHEET_ID_DISPATCH_V2 (dibuat otomatis lengkap dengan header kalau
 * belum ada). Seluruh isian checklist (SPAREPART/PRE-RTD CHECK/dst) dicatat
 * sebagai satu kolom JSON supaya sheet tetap ringkas walau jumlah & jenis
 * item checklist berbeda-beda per sheet asal. Kalau data.rowNumberSource
 * (nomor baris di sheet "Task Machine Preparation") disertakan, PIC (dari
 * dropdown di Popup) ditulis ke kolom K sheet tsb, dan baris sumbernya
 * langsung ditandai STATUS "Done" supaya card-nya hilang dari Cardview Team
 * Prepare.
 */
function submitTeamPrepareTask_(data) {
  data = data || {};
  var noVMC = String(data.noVMC || '').trim();
  if (!noVMC) {
    return { success: false, message: 'VMC NO wajib diisi.' };
  }
  var pic = String(data.pic || '').trim();
  if (!pic) {
    return { success: false, message: 'PIC wajib dipilih.' };
  }

  // 1) Tulis balik ke spreadsheet sumber (Inventory JS): FINAL STATUS (K),
  //    KETERANGAN (BU), dan seluruh checklist (SPAREPART/PRE-RTD CHECK/LCD &
  //    Touchscreen/Coffee Maker/dst) langsung ke kolom aslinya, di baris yang
  //    cocok VMC NO + EX. LOCATION. Kalau ini gagal (baris tidak ketemu),
  //    seluruh proses simpan dibatalkan (tidak lanjut ke log di bawah).
  var writeBackRes = _writeTeamPrepareBackToSource_(data);
  if (!writeBackRes.success) {
    return writeBackRes;
  }

  // 2) Catat juga sebagai log riwayat pengerjaan (histori lengkap tiap kali
  //    Popup disimpan, termasuk snapshot checklist dalam JSON) di sheet
  //    "Task Team Prepare" pada spreadsheet Dashboard Dispatch V2.
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_DISPATCH_V2);
  var sheet = _getOrCreateTeamPrepareResultSheet_(ss);

  var timestamp = new Date();
  // data.photoUrls: array URL Drive hasil upload satu-per-satu lewat action
  // 'uploadTeamPreparePhoto' (dipanggil dari frontend SEBELUM action ini),
  // digabung dengan newline supaya konsisten dengan pola "Foto Bukti" di
  // sheet "Task Team Dispatch" (lihat _driveThumbnailSrc_/parsing di frontend).
  var row = [
    timestamp,
    noVMC,
    data.serialNumber || '',
    data.machineType || '',
    data.exLocation || '',
    data.machineDetail || '',
    data.inboundDate || '',
    data.assets || '',
    data.alokasi || '',
    data.finalStatus || '',
    data.keterangan || '',
    data.pic || '',
    data.sheetAsal || '',
    JSON.stringify(data.checklist || []),
    (data.photoUrls || []).filter(Boolean).join('\n')
  ];

  var startRow = Math.max(sheet.getLastRow() + 1, 2);
  sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, 1, TEAM_PREPARE_RESULT_HEADERS.length).setValues([row]);

  var sourceUpdateMessage = '';
  var rowNumberSource = Number(data.rowNumberSource);
  if (rowNumberSource && rowNumberSource >= 2) {
    var picRes = _setTaskMachinePrepPic_(ss, rowNumberSource, pic);
    if (!picRes.success) {
      sourceUpdateMessage += ' (peringatan: gagal menyimpan PIC ke Task Machine Preparation — ' + picRes.message + ')';
    }
    var updRes = updateTaskMachinePrepStatus_({ rowNumber: rowNumberSource, status: 'Done' });
    if (!updRes.success) {
      sourceUpdateMessage += ' (peringatan: gagal menandai task sumber Done — ' + updRes.message + ')';
    }
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    message: 'Data pengerjaan "' + noVMC + '" berhasil disimpan ke sheet "' + writeBackRes.sheetAsal +
      '" (baris ' + writeBackRes.rowNumber + ') dan dicatat di log "' + SHEET_NAME_TEAM_PREPARE_RESULT + '".' + sourceUpdateMessage,
    row: startRow
  };
}

/**
 * Ambil seluruh riwayat hasil pengerjaan Team Prepare dari sheet
 * "Task Team Prepare" (SPREADSHEET_ID_DISPATCH_V2) untuk menu
 * "Historycal Preparation" (submenu di bawah Machine Preparation). Setiap
 * baris disertai "rowNumber" (posisi baris di sheet, 1-based) supaya bisa
 * dipakai kalau nanti perlu update/detail lanjutan. Sama seperti
 * getTaskTeamDispatchHistory_: seluruh kolom dikembalikan (termasuk DETAIL
 * CHECKLIST (JSON) & FOTO BUKTI) supaya popup detail (klik VMC NO) bisa
 * dirender murni dari data yang sudah dimuat sekali, tanpa fetch tambahan.
 */
function getTeamPrepareHistory_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_DISPATCH_V2);
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME_TEAM_PREPARE_RESULT);
  if (!sheet) {
    return { success: true, rows: [] };
  }

  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) {
    return { success: true, rows: [] };
  }

  var numRows = lastRow - 1;
  var values = sheet.getRange(2, 1, numRows, TEAM_PREPARE_RESULT_HEADERS.length).getValues();
  var timestampColIdx = TEAM_PREPARE_RESULT_HEADERS.indexOf('Timestamp');

  var rows = [];
  values.forEach(function (rowVals, idx) {
    var hasData = rowVals.some(function (cell) { return cell !== '' && cell !== null; });
    if (!hasData) return;

    var obj = { rowNumber: idx + 2 };
    TEAM_PREPARE_RESULT_HEADERS.forEach(function (col, colIdx) {
      var val = rowVals[colIdx];
      if (colIdx === timestampColIdx && val instanceof Date) {
        val = Utilities.formatDate(val, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
      }
      obj[col] = (val === null ? '' : val);
    });
    rows.push(obj);
  });

  // Terbaru dulu (Timestamp desc).
  rows.sort(function (a, b) {
    return new Date(b['Timestamp'] || 0).getTime() - new Date(a['Timestamp'] || 0).getTime();
  });

  return { success: true, rows: rows };
}


/**
 * ============================================================
 *  MENU UTAMA - CARDVIEW "Status Order from BD"
 *  Sumber: sheet "Jabodetabek" (spreadsheet "Logistic Request 2026",
 *  SPREADSHEET_ID_JABODETABEK), kriteria status dari kolom AB
 *  (STATUS_COL_JABODETABEK = "LOGISTIC UPDATE"). Kosong -> "Open".
 *  Hanya baris tahun berjalan (dihitung dari kolom TIMESTAMP = kolom B,
 *  DATE_COL_JABODETABEK) yang ikut dihitung/ditampilkan. Popup detail per
 *  status berisi BD PIC, ACTION PLAN (VM TYPE ON SITE kalau ACTION PLAN =
 *  Withdrawal), dan LOCATION NAME, diurut TIMESTAMP terbaru di atas.
 * ============================================================
 */
var BD_STATUS_LIST = [
  'Open',
  'Done',
  'VM Not Ready',
  'Waiting Event Finish',
  'Waiting Pick Up',
  'Waiting List Delivery',
  'Waiting for Relocation',
  'In Progress',
  'Pending by PIC',
  'Pending by Sales',
  'Pending Sparepart',
  'Pending Outstanding',
  'Pending Access',
  'Pending by Partner (Otsuka/Nutrifood)',
  'Hold',
  'Cancel',
  'Done Take Out - Waiting for Exchange',
  'Delivered - Pending Activation'
];

function _bdStatusDateMs_(val) {
  if (val instanceof Date) {
    return isNaN(val.getTime()) ? -Infinity : val.getTime();
  }
  if (val === '' || val === null || val === undefined) return -Infinity;
  var parsed = new Date(val);
  return isNaN(parsed.getTime()) ? -Infinity : parsed.getTime();
}

function _bdStatusYear_(val) {
  if (val instanceof Date) {
    return isNaN(val.getTime()) ? null : val.getFullYear();
  }
  if (val === '' || val === null || val === undefined) return null;
  var parsed = new Date(val);
  return isNaN(parsed.getTime()) ? null : parsed.getFullYear();
}

function getBDStatusSummary_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_JABODETABEK);
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME_JABODETABEK);

  if (!sheet) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Sheet "' + SHEET_NAME_JABODETABEK + '" tidak ditemukan' };
  }

  var currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();

  var headersAW = sheet
    .getRange(HEADER_ROW_JABODETABEK, 1, 1, LAST_COL_JABODETABEK)
    .getValues()[0]
    .map(function (h) { return h === '' || h === null ? '' : String(h).trim(); });

  function colIdx_(name) {
    return headersAW.findIndex(function (h) { return h.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase(); });
  }

  var bdPicIdx = colIdx_('BD PIC');
  var actionPlanIdx = colIdx_('ACTION PLAN');
  var vmTypeOnSiteIdx = colIdx_('VM TYPE ON SITE');
  var proposedVmTypeIdx = colIdx_('PROPOSED VM TYPE');
  var locationNameIdx = colIdx_('LOCATION NAME');

  var counts = {};
  var details = {};
  BD_STATUS_LIST.forEach(function (st) { counts[st] = 0; details[st] = []; });

  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow >= DATA_START_ROW_JABODETABEK) {
    var numDataRows = lastRow - DATA_START_ROW_JABODETABEK + 1;

    var displayValues = sheet
      .getRange(DATA_START_ROW_JABODETABEK, 1, numDataRows, LAST_COL_JABODETABEK)
      .getValues();

    var statusValues = sheet
      .getRange(DATA_START_ROW_JABODETABEK, STATUS_COL_JABODETABEK, numDataRows, 1)
      .getValues();

    for (var i = 0; i < displayValues.length; i++) {
      var rowVals = displayValues[i];
      var hasData = rowVals.some(function (cell) { return cell !== '' && cell !== null; });
      if (!hasData) continue;

      var dateCell = rowVals[DATE_COL_JABODETABEK - 1];
      var year = _bdStatusYear_(dateCell);
      if (year !== currentYear) continue;

      var statusRaw = statusValues[i][0];
      var status = (statusRaw === '' || statusRaw === null) ? '' : String(statusRaw).trim();
      var statusKey = status === '' ? 'Open' : status;
      if (BD_STATUS_LIST.indexOf(statusKey) === -1) continue;

      var actionPlanVal = actionPlanIdx === -1 ? '' : (rowVals[actionPlanIdx] || '');
      var vmTypeOnSiteVal = vmTypeOnSiteIdx === -1 ? '' : (rowVals[vmTypeOnSiteIdx] || '');
      var proposedVmTypeVal = proposedVmTypeIdx === -1 ? '' : (rowVals[proposedVmTypeIdx] || '');

      counts[statusKey]++;
      details[statusKey].push({
        bdPic: bdPicIdx === -1 ? '' : (rowVals[bdPicIdx] || ''),
        actionPlan: actionPlanVal,
        vmTypeOnSite: vmTypeOnSiteVal,
        proposedVmType: proposedVmTypeVal,
        locationName: locationNameIdx === -1 ? '' : (rowVals[locationNameIdx] || ''),
        dateMs: _bdStatusDateMs_(dateCell)
      });
    }
  }

  BD_STATUS_LIST.forEach(function (st) {
    details[st].sort(function (a, b) { return b.dateMs - a.dateMs; });
  });

  return { success: true, year: currentYear, statusList: BD_STATUS_LIST, counts: counts, details: details };
}

/**
 * ============================================================
 *  MENU UTAMA - CARDVIEW "Action Plan"
 *  Ditaruh persis di bawah cardview "Status Order from BD".
 *  Sumber data dari sheet "Jabodetabek" (spreadsheet "Logistic Request
 *  2026"), sama seperti getBDStatusSummary_: hanya baris tahun berjalan
 *  (kolom TIMESTAMP = DATE_COL_JABODETABEK) yang ikut dihitung. BEDANYA
 *  dengan getBDStatusSummary_: di sini HANYA baris dengan status (kolom AB,
 *  STATUS_COL_JABODETABEK) = "Open" (kosong dianggap "Open") yang dihitung
 *  dan dikelompokkan per kolom ACTION PLAN. Badge yang ditampilkan cuma 4 tipe:
 *  Delivery Approval, Withdrawal, Machine Exchange, Relocation. Tipe lain
 *  (kalau ada) tetap dihitung supaya tidak hilang, tapi tidak dibuatkan
 *  badge tersendiri di frontend.
 * ============================================================
 */
var ACTION_PLAN_LIST = [
  'Delivery Approval',
  'Withdrawal',
  'Machine Exchange',
  'Relocation'
];

function getActionPlanSummary_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_JABODETABEK);
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME_JABODETABEK);

  if (!sheet) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Sheet "' + SHEET_NAME_JABODETABEK + '" tidak ditemukan' };
  }

  var currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();

  var headersAW = sheet
    .getRange(HEADER_ROW_JABODETABEK, 1, 1, LAST_COL_JABODETABEK)
    .getValues()[0]
    .map(function (h) { return h === '' || h === null ? '' : String(h).trim(); });

  function colIdx_(name) {
    return headersAW.findIndex(function (h) { return h.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase(); });
  }

  var bdPicIdx = colIdx_('BD PIC');
  var actionPlanIdx = colIdx_('ACTION PLAN');
  var vmTypeOnSiteIdx = colIdx_('VM TYPE ON SITE');
  var proposedVmTypeIdx = colIdx_('PROPOSED VM TYPE');
  var locationNameIdx = colIdx_('LOCATION NAME');

  var counts = {};
  var details = {};
  ACTION_PLAN_LIST.forEach(function (ap) { counts[ap] = 0; details[ap] = []; });

  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow >= DATA_START_ROW_JABODETABEK) {
    var numDataRows = lastRow - DATA_START_ROW_JABODETABEK + 1;

    var displayValues = sheet
      .getRange(DATA_START_ROW_JABODETABEK, 1, numDataRows, LAST_COL_JABODETABEK)
      .getValues();

    var statusValues = sheet
      .getRange(DATA_START_ROW_JABODETABEK, STATUS_COL_JABODETABEK, numDataRows, 1)
      .getValues();

    for (var i = 0; i < displayValues.length; i++) {
      var rowVals = displayValues[i];
      var hasData = rowVals.some(function (cell) { return cell !== '' && cell !== null; });
      if (!hasData) continue;

      var dateCell = rowVals[DATE_COL_JABODETABEK - 1];
      var year = _bdStatusYear_(dateCell);
      if (year !== currentYear) continue;

      var statusRaw = statusValues[i][0];
      var status = (statusRaw === '' || statusRaw === null) ? '' : String(statusRaw).trim();
      var statusKey = status === '' ? 'Open' : status;
      if (statusKey !== 'Open') continue;

      var actionPlanRaw = actionPlanIdx === -1 ? '' : (rowVals[actionPlanIdx] || '');
      var actionPlanVal = String(actionPlanRaw || '').trim();
      if (!actionPlanVal || ACTION_PLAN_LIST.indexOf(actionPlanVal) === -1) continue;

      var vmTypeOnSiteVal = vmTypeOnSiteIdx === -1 ? '' : (rowVals[vmTypeOnSiteIdx] || '');
      var proposedVmTypeVal = proposedVmTypeIdx === -1 ? '' : (rowVals[proposedVmTypeIdx] || '');

      counts[actionPlanVal]++;
      details[actionPlanVal].push({
        bdPic: bdPicIdx === -1 ? '' : (rowVals[bdPicIdx] || ''),
        status: statusKey,
        vmTypeOnSite: vmTypeOnSiteVal,
        proposedVmType: proposedVmTypeVal,
        locationName: locationNameIdx === -1 ? '' : (rowVals[locationNameIdx] || ''),
        dateMs: _bdStatusDateMs_(dateCell)
      });
    }
  }

  ACTION_PLAN_LIST.forEach(function (ap) {
    details[ap].sort(function (a, b) { return b.dateMs - a.dateMs; });
  });

  return { success: true, year: currentYear, actionPlanList: ACTION_PLAN_LIST, counts: counts, details: details };
}

/**
 * ============================================================
 *  MENU UTAMA - "Summary Dispatch"
 *  Ditaruh persis di bawah cardview "Action Plan". Sumber data GABUNGAN
 *  dari sheet "Jabodetabek" DAN "Event" (spreadsheet "Logistic Request
 *  2026", SPREADSHEET_ID_JABODETABEK). Hanya baris dengan status "Done"
 *  yang dihitung — kolom status yang jadi acuan "Done" per sheet TETAP
 *  pakai konstanta yang sudah ada di file ini (STATUS_COL_JABODETABEK =
 *  kolom AB "LOGISTICS UPDATE" untuk sheet "Jabodetabek", STATUS_COL_EVENT
 *  = kolom T untuk sheet "Event"), dan tahun TIMESTAMP-nya = tahun berjalan.
 *
 *  Kolom ACTION PLAN, Timestamp, dan PIC Dispatch dicari secara DINAMIS
 *  lewat nama header (bukan angka kolom tetap) supaya tetap jalan walau
 *  posisi kolom di sheet "Event" berbeda dari "Jabodetabek".
 *
 *  Mengembalikan:
 *   - months        : label bulan (Jan .. bulan berjalan)
 *   - actionPlanList: 4 tipe ACTION_PLAN_LIST (sama dgn cardview "Action Plan")
 *   - series        : { 'Delivery Approval': [qty per bulan], ... } — dipakai
 *                      grafik tren, difilter di frontend (Semua/per tipe)
 *   - picTable      : [{ pic, qty }, ...] diurut qty terbesar. Kalau kolom
 *                      PIC Dispatch berisi lebih dari satu nama (dipisah
 *                      koma, format "team"), SETIAP nama dihitung — konsisten
 *                      dengan cara Task Team Dispatch/Preview-Share
 *                      memperlakukan PIC multi-nama sebagai satu team yang
 *                      seluruh anggotanya ikut dihitung.
 * ============================================================
 */
var SUMMARY_DISPATCH_HEADER_SCAN_WIDTH = 40; // lebar baca header (kolom) untuk cari ACTION PLAN/Timestamp/PIC Dispatch

function _summaryDispatchColIdx_(headers, name) {
  var target = String(name).trim().toLowerCase();
  return headers.findIndex(function (h) { return String(h || '').trim().toLowerCase() === target; });
}

// Ambil seluruh baris status "Done" (tahun berjalan) dari satu sheet, dalam
// bentuk { actionPlan, monthIdx (0-11), picRaw } siap digabung antar-sheet.
function _summaryDispatchExtractDone_(sheet, statusCol, currentYear) {
  var out = [];
  if (!sheet) return out;

  var maxCols = sheet.getMaxColumns();
  var headerWidth = Math.min(Math.max(SUMMARY_DISPATCH_HEADER_SCAN_WIDTH, statusCol), maxCols);

  var headers = sheet
    .getRange(1, 1, 1, headerWidth)
    .getValues()[0]
    .map(function (h) { return h === '' || h === null ? '' : String(h).trim(); });

  var actionPlanIdx = _summaryDispatchColIdx_(headers, 'ACTION PLAN');
  var timestampIdx = _summaryDispatchColIdx_(headers, 'Timestamp');
  var picIdx = _summaryDispatchColIdx_(headers, 'PIC Dispatch');

  // Tanpa kolom ACTION PLAN atau Timestamp, baris sheet ini tidak bisa
  // diklasifikasikan per bulan/aktivitas — dilewati (bukan error fatal,
  // supaya sheet lain tetap diproses).
  if (actionPlanIdx === -1 || timestampIdx === -1) return out;

  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return out;

  var numDataRows = lastRow - 1;
  var readWidth = Math.min(Math.max(headerWidth, statusCol), maxCols);
  var values = sheet.getRange(2, 1, numDataRows, readWidth).getValues();

  for (var i = 0; i < values.length; i++) {
    var rowVals = values[i];
    var hasData = rowVals.some(function (cell) { return cell !== '' && cell !== null; });
    if (!hasData) continue;

    var statusRaw = rowVals[statusCol - 1];
    var status = String(statusRaw || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (status !== 'done') continue;

    var dateCell = rowVals[timestampIdx];
    var dateObj = dateCell instanceof Date ? dateCell : new Date(dateCell);
    if (isNaN(dateObj.getTime())) continue;
    if (dateObj.getFullYear() !== currentYear) continue;

    var actionPlanVal = String(rowVals[actionPlanIdx] || '').trim();
    if (!actionPlanVal || ACTION_PLAN_LIST.indexOf(actionPlanVal) === -1) continue;

    out.push({
      actionPlan: actionPlanVal,
      monthIdx: dateObj.getMonth(),
      // dateStr (yyyy-MM-dd) dikirim ke client supaya grafik & tabel PIC Dispatch
      // bisa dikelompokkan ulang per HARI/MINGGU tanpa perlu round-trip server lagi
      // (lihat filter "Bulan/Minggu/Hari" di menu Summary Dispatch, index.html).
      dateStr: Utilities.formatDate(dateObj, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd'),
      picRaw: picIdx === -1 ? '' : String(rowVals[picIdx] || '').trim()
    });
  }

  return out;
}

var SUMMARY_DISPATCH_MONTH_NAMES = [
  'Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'Mei', 'Jun',
  'Jul', 'Agu', 'Sep', 'Okt', 'Nov', 'Des'
];

function getSummaryDispatch_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_JABODETABEK);
  var sheetJbdtbk = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME_JABODETABEK);
  var sheetEvent = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME_EVENT);

  if (!sheetJbdtbk && !sheetEvent) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Sheet "' + SHEET_NAME_JABODETABEK + '" dan "' + SHEET_NAME_EVENT + '" tidak ditemukan' };
  }

  var currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();
  var currentMonth = new Date().getMonth(); // 0-based, bulan berjalan

  var rowsJbdtbk = _summaryDispatchExtractDone_(sheetJbdtbk, STATUS_COL_JABODETABEK, currentYear);
  var rowsEvent = _summaryDispatchExtractDone_(sheetEvent, STATUS_COL_EVENT, currentYear);
  var allRows = rowsJbdtbk.concat(rowsEvent);

  // --- Tren bulanan per ACTION PLAN (Jan s.d. bulan berjalan) ---
  var months = SUMMARY_DISPATCH_MONTH_NAMES.slice(0, currentMonth + 1);
  var series = {};
  ACTION_PLAN_LIST.forEach(function (ap) {
    series[ap] = months.map(function () { return 0; });
  });
  allRows.forEach(function (r) {
    if (r.monthIdx > currentMonth) return; // jaga-jaga data lompat ke bulan depan
    series[r.actionPlan][r.monthIdx]++;
  });

  // --- Tabel PIC Dispatch: qty activity Done PER BULAN (Jan s.d. bulan
  // berjalan), bukan total keseluruhan, supaya kelihatan naik/turunnya
  // tiap PIC per bulan. ---
  var picMonthly = {};
  allRows.forEach(function (r) {
    if (!r.picRaw || r.monthIdx > currentMonth) return;
    r.picRaw.split(',').forEach(function (name) {
      var n = name.trim();
      if (!n) return;
      if (!picMonthly[n]) picMonthly[n] = months.map(function () { return 0; });
      picMonthly[n][r.monthIdx]++;
    });
  });
  var picTable = Object.keys(picMonthly)
    .map(function (name) {
      var monthly = picMonthly[name];
      var total = monthly.reduce(function (sum, v) { return sum + v; }, 0);
      return { pic: name, monthly: monthly, total: total };
    })
    .sort(function (a, b) { return b.total - a.total || a.pic.localeCompare(b.pic); });

  return {
    success: true,
    year: currentYear,
    months: months,
    actionPlanList: ACTION_PLAN_LIST,
    series: series,
    picTable: picTable,
    // Data mentah (per baris Done, tahun berjalan) supaya frontend bisa
    // menghitung ulang grafik & tabel PIC Dispatch per HARI/MINGGU (bukan cuma
    // per bulan) tanpa panggilan server tambahan — lihat
    // _summaryDispatchRegroupByGranularity_ di index.html.
    rawRows: allRows
  };
}

function getInventoryJS_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_INVENTORY_JS);
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME_INVENTORY_JS);

  if (!sheet) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Sheet "' + SHEET_NAME_INVENTORY_JS + '" tidak ditemukan' };
  }

  var headers = sheet
    .getRange(HEADER_ROW_INVENTORY_JS, 1, 1, LAST_COL_INVENTORY_JS)
    .getValues()[0]
    .map(function (h) { return h === '' || h === null ? '' : String(h).trim(); });

  var numDataRows = DATA_END_ROW_INVENTORY_JS - DATA_START_ROW_INVENTORY_JS + 1;
  var values = sheet
    .getRange(DATA_START_ROW_INVENTORY_JS, 1, numDataRows, LAST_COL_INVENTORY_JS)
    .getValues();

  var data = values
    .filter(function (row) {
      return row.some(function (cell) { return cell !== '' && cell !== null; });
    })
    .map(function (row) {
      var obj = {};
      headers.forEach(function (h, i) {
        var key = h || ('Kolom' + (i + 1));
        obj[key] = row[i];
      });
      return obj;
    });

  return { success: true, headers: headers, data: data };
}

/**
 * Ambil data Inventory Machine CIMB.
 * Header tetap dari baris 2 kolom J:Q (J2:Q2), data tetap dari baris 3 s.d. 38 (J3:Q38).
 * Baris yang seluruh selnya kosong akan dilewati.
 */
function getInventoryCIMB_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_INVENTORY_CIMB);
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME_INVENTORY_CIMB);

  if (!sheet) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Sheet "' + SHEET_NAME_INVENTORY_CIMB + '" tidak ditemukan' };
  }

  var numCols = LAST_COL_INVENTORY_CIMB - FIRST_COL_INVENTORY_CIMB + 1;

  var headers = sheet
    .getRange(HEADER_ROW_INVENTORY_CIMB, FIRST_COL_INVENTORY_CIMB, 1, numCols)
    .getValues()[0]
    .map(function (h) { return h === '' || h === null ? '' : String(h).trim(); });

  var numDataRows = DATA_END_ROW_INVENTORY_CIMB - DATA_START_ROW_INVENTORY_CIMB + 1;
  var values = sheet
    .getRange(DATA_START_ROW_INVENTORY_CIMB, FIRST_COL_INVENTORY_CIMB, numDataRows, numCols)
    .getValues();

  var data = values
    .filter(function (row) {
      return row.some(function (cell) { return cell !== '' && cell !== null; });
    })
    .map(function (row) {
      var obj = {};
      headers.forEach(function (h, i) {
        var key = h || ('Kolom' + (i + 1));
        obj[key] = row[i];
      });
      return obj;
    });

  return { success: true, headers: headers, data: data };
}

/**
 * Ambil semua sumber dropdown untuk form Stock Opname dari sheet "Source".
 * Baris kosong pada satu kolom dilewati (tidak ikut masuk ke array hasil).
 *   - Machine Detail : A1:A41
 *   - Machine Type   : B1:B41
 *   - Alokasi        : F1:F6
 *   - Final Status   : G1:G7
 *   - Warehouse      : J1:J5
 *   - Assets         : O1:O4
 */
function getSourceDropdowns_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_INVENTORY_JS);
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME_SOURCE);

  if (!sheet) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Sheet "' + SHEET_NAME_SOURCE + '" tidak ditemukan' };
  }

  function readColumn_(a1Range) {
    var values = sheet.getRange(a1Range).getValues();
    var out = [];
    values.forEach(function (row) {
      var v = row[0];
      if (v !== '' && v !== null && v !== undefined) {
        out.push(String(v).trim());
      }
    });
    return out;
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    machineDetail: readColumn_('A1:A41'),
    machineType: readColumn_('B1:B41'),
    alokasi: readColumn_('F1:F6'),
    finalStatus: readColumn_('G1:G7'),
    warehouse: readColumn_('J1:J5'),
    assets: readColumn_('O1:O4')
  };
}


/**
 * ============================================================
 *  REQUEST BD — FORM BOOKING MACHINE + MACHINE BOOKED
 *  Action GET: getBookingFormData, getMachineBooked
 *  Action tulis (GET+requestId, dijaga _handleGuardedWrite_): submitMachineBooking, cancelMachineBooking
 *  Cancelled  : Status -> "Cancelled" + kolom "Cancelled At"; hilang dari tampilan 1 bulan
 *               setelah dibatalkan (baris sheet tetap ada). Tiket & mesin bebas lagi.
 *  Delivered  : booking Booked yang TICKET-nya LOGISTICS UPDATE (AB) = "Done" tampil "Delivered".
 *
 *  Penyimpanan : sheet "Machine Booked" di "Dashboard Dispatch V2"
 *                (SPREADSHEET_ID_DISPATCH_V2) — dibuat otomatis kalau belum ada.
 *  BD list     : sheet "Source" kolom A2:A di "Logistic Request 2026"
 *  Lokasi      : sheet "Jabodetabek", LOGISTICS UPDATE (kolom AB) masih kosong,
 *                BD PIC = BD terpilih, ACTION PLAN = Delivery Approval / Machine Exchange
 *  Mesin       : FINAL STATUS = "RTD" di 5 sheet STOCK_OPNAME_ALLOWED_SHEETS
 *  Anti-double : (1) requestId sekali pakai, (2) script lock, (3) validasi ulang
 *                di server: tiket belum Booked, mesin belum Booked, tiket masih
 *                kosong di AB, mesin masih RTD.
 *  Mesin yang berstatus "Booked" TIDAK dihitung sebagai RTD di Dashboard,
 *  Machine Preparation, dan dropdown RTD Input Task.
 * ============================================================
 */
var SHEET_NAME_MACHINE_BOOKED = 'Machine Booked';
var BOOKING_ACTION_PLANS = ['Delivery Approval', 'Machine Exchange'];
var MACHINE_BOOKED_HEADERS = [
  'Timestamp', 'BD PIC', 'Action Plan', 'TICKET', 'Location Name', 'NEW VM POSITION',
  'NO VMC', 'Machine Type', 'Akses', 'PKS', 'Permit Loading', 'Konfirmasi PIC Site',
  'Status', 'Booking ID', 'Sheet Asal', 'Serial Number', 'Cancelled At'
];

function _bkNorm_(v) { return String(v === null || v === undefined ? '' : v).trim().toLowerCase(); }

// Kunci unik mesin: sheet asal + NO VMC + serial. Dipakai konsisten di semua fungsi.
function _bkMachineKey_(sheetName, noVMC, serial) {
  return String(sheetName) + '||' + String(noVMC || '').trim() + '||' + String(serial || '').trim();
}

function _getOrCreateMachineBookedSheet_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_DISPATCH_V2);
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME_MACHINE_BOOKED);
  if (!sheet) sheet = ss.insertSheet(SHEET_NAME_MACHINE_BOOKED);
  if (sheet.getMaxColumns() < MACHINE_BOOKED_HEADERS.length) {
    sheet.insertColumnsAfter(sheet.getMaxColumns(), MACHINE_BOOKED_HEADERS.length - sheet.getMaxColumns());
  }
  var cur = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, MACHINE_BOOKED_HEADERS.length).getValues()[0];
  var same = cur.every(function (h, i) { return String(h).trim() === MACHINE_BOOKED_HEADERS[i]; });
  if (!same) {
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, MACHINE_BOOKED_HEADERS.length).setValues([MACHINE_BOOKED_HEADERS]);
    sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
  }
  return sheet;
}

// Baca semua baris Machine Booked sebagai array of object (key = header).
function _readMachineBookedRows_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_DISPATCH_V2);
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME_MACHINE_BOOKED);
  if (!sheet) return [];
  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return [];
  var width = Math.min(MACHINE_BOOKED_HEADERS.length, sheet.getMaxColumns());
  var values = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, width).getValues();
  var rows = [];
  values.forEach(function (r) {
    if (!r.some(function (c) { return c !== '' && c !== null; })) return;
    var obj = {};
    MACHINE_BOOKED_HEADERS.forEach(function (h, i) {
      var v = i < r.length ? r[i] : '';
      if (v instanceof Date) v = Utilities.formatDate(v, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
      obj[h] = v === null || v === undefined ? '' : v;
    });
    rows.push(obj);
  });
  return rows;
}

// Set kunci mesin & tiket yang SEDANG Booked (dipakai untuk menyembunyikan dari RTD).
function _getBookedSets_() {
  var machines = {}, tickets = {};
  _readMachineBookedRows_().forEach(function (r) {
    if (_bkNorm_(r['Status']) !== 'booked') return;
    machines[_bkMachineKey_(r['Sheet Asal'], r['NO VMC'], r['Serial Number'])] = true;
    var t = _bkNorm_(r['TICKET']);
    if (t) tickets[t] = true;
  });
  return { machines: machines, tickets: tickets };
}

// Dipakai fungsi RTD lain: true kalau baris mesin ini sedang Booked.
function _isMachineBooked_(bookedSets, sheetName, row) {
  return !!bookedSets.machines[_bkMachineKey_(
    sheetName,
    row[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.noVMC - 1],
    row[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.serialNumber - 1]
  )];
}

// Daftar mesin RTD yang belum Booked.
function _bkListRtdMachines_(bookedSets) {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_INVENTORY_JS);
  var out = [];
  STOCK_OPNAME_ALLOWED_SHEETS.forEach(function (sheetName) {
    var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName);
    if (!sheet) return;
    var lastRow = _stockOpnameLastDataRow_(sheet);
    var numRows = lastRow - STOCK_OPNAME_DATA_START_ROW + 1;
    if (numRows <= 0) return;
    var values = sheet.getRange(STOCK_OPNAME_DATA_START_ROW, 1, numRows, 13).getValues();
    values.forEach(function (row) {
      if (String(row[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.finalStatus - 1] || '').trim() !== 'RTD') return;
      var noVMC = String(row[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.noVMC - 1] || '').trim();
      var serial = String(row[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.serialNumber - 1] || '').trim();
      if (!noVMC && !serial) return;
      if (_isMachineBooked_(bookedSets, sheetName, row)) return;
      var machineType = String(row[STOCK_OPNAME_COLS.machineType - 1] || '').trim();
      out.push({
        key: _bkMachineKey_(sheetName, noVMC, serial),
        label: [noVMC || '(tanpa NO VMC)', machineType, serial ? 'SN ' + serial : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' — '),
        noVMC: noVMC, serialNumber: serial, machineType: machineType, sheet: sheetName
      });
    });
  });
  return out;
}

// Daftar lokasi (tiket Jabodetabek) yang AB-nya masih kosong & belum Booked.
function _bkListOpenLocations_(bookedSets) {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_JABODETABEK);
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME_JABODETABEK);
  if (!sheet) return { error: 'Sheet "' + SHEET_NAME_JABODETABEK + '" tidak ditemukan' };
  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < DATA_START_ROW_JABODETABEK) return { rows: [] };

  var headers = sheet.getRange(HEADER_ROW_JABODETABEK, 1, 1, LAST_COL_JABODETABEK).getValues()[0]
    .map(function (h) { return String(h === null ? '' : h).trim().toLowerCase(); });
  function idx(name, fallback) { var i = headers.indexOf(name.toLowerCase()); return i === -1 ? fallback : i; }
  var iTicket = idx('TICKET', 0);
  var iBd = idx('BD PIC', 1);
  var iAction = idx('ACTION PLAN', -1);
  var iLoc = idx('LOCATION NAME', -1);
  var iNewVm = idx('NEW VM POSITION', 8); // fallback kolom I (sesuai spesifikasi)
  if (iAction === -1 || iLoc === -1) return { error: 'Header ACTION PLAN / LOCATION NAME tidak ditemukan di sheet Jabodetabek' };

  var n = lastRow - DATA_START_ROW_JABODETABEK + 1;
  var vals = sheet.getRange(DATA_START_ROW_JABODETABEK, 1, n, LAST_COL_JABODETABEK).getValues();
  var ab = sheet.getRange(DATA_START_ROW_JABODETABEK, STATUS_COL_JABODETABEK, n, 1).getValues();

  var rows = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < vals.length; i++) {
    var r = vals[i];
    if (String(ab[i][0] === null ? '' : ab[i][0]).trim() !== '') continue; // AB harus kosong
    var ticket = String(r[iTicket] || '').trim();
    var actionPlan = String(r[iAction] || '').trim();
    if (!ticket || BOOKING_ACTION_PLANS.indexOf(actionPlan) === -1) continue;
    if (bookedSets.tickets[_bkNorm_(ticket)]) continue;
    var loc = String(r[iLoc] || '').trim();
    var newVm = String(r[iNewVm] || '').trim();
    rows.push({
      ticket: ticket, bd: String(r[iBd] || '').trim(), actionPlan: actionPlan,
      locationName: loc, newVmPosition: newVm,
      label: newVm ? (loc + ' - ' + newVm) : loc
    });
  }
  return { rows: rows };
}

function getBookingFormData_() {
  var bookedSets = _getBookedSets_();

  var ssJ = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_JABODETABEK);
  var src = ssJ.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME_JABODETABEK_SOURCE);
  if (!src) return { success: false, message: 'Sheet "' + SHEET_NAME_JABODETABEK_SOURCE + '" tidak ditemukan di Logistic Request 2026' };
  var bdList = [], seen = {};
  if (src.getLastRow() >= 2) {
    src.getRange(2, 1, src.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues().forEach(function (r) {
      var v = String(r[0] === null ? '' : r[0]).trim();
      if (v && !seen[v.toLowerCase()]) { seen[v.toLowerCase()] = true; bdList.push(v); }
    });
  }

  var locs = _bkListOpenLocations_(bookedSets);
  if (locs.error) return { success: false, message: locs.error };

  return {
    success: true,
    bdList: bdList,
    locations: locs.rows,
    machines: _bkListRtdMachines_(bookedSets)
  };
}

// Peta TICKET -> nilai LOGISTICS UPDATE (kolom AB) di sheet Jabodetabek (huruf kecil, trim).
function _bkGetLogisticsUpdateMap_() {
  var map = {};
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID_JABODETABEK);
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME_JABODETABEK);
  if (!sheet) return map;
  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < DATA_START_ROW_JABODETABEK) return map;
  var headers = sheet.getRange(HEADER_ROW_JABODETABEK, 1, 1, LAST_COL_JABODETABEK).getValues()[0]
    .map(function (h) { return String(h === null ? '' : h).trim().toLowerCase(); });
  var iTicket = headers.indexOf('ticket');
  if (iTicket === -1) iTicket = 0;
  var n = lastRow - DATA_START_ROW_JABODETABEK + 1;
  var tickets = sheet.getRange(DATA_START_ROW_JABODETABEK, iTicket + 1, n, 1).getValues();
  var ab = sheet.getRange(DATA_START_ROW_JABODETABEK, STATUS_COL_JABODETABEK, n, 1).getValues();
  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    var t = _bkNorm_(tickets[i][0]);
    if (t) map[t] = _bkNorm_(ab[i][0]);
  }
  return map;
}

// "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss" (hasil _readMachineBookedRows_) -> Date lokal; null kalau tidak valid.
function _bkParseDate_(str) {
  if (!str) return null;
  var d = new Date(String(str).replace(' ', 'T'));
  return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d;
}

var MACHINE_BOOKED_CANCELLED_VISIBLE_MONTHS = 1;

function getMachineBooked_() {
  var rows = _readMachineBookedRows_();
  var lu = _bkGetLogisticsUpdateMap_();
  var cutoff = new Date();
  cutoff.setMonth(cutoff.getMonth() - MACHINE_BOOKED_CANCELLED_VISIBLE_MONTHS);

  var out = [];
  rows.forEach(function (r) {
    var st = _bkNorm_(r['Status']);
    if (st === 'cancelled') {
      // Cancelled disembunyikan 1 bulan setelah dibatalkan (baris di sheet tetap disimpan).
      var d = _bkParseDate_(r['Cancelled At']) || _bkParseDate_(r['Timestamp']);
      if (d && d < cutoff) return;
    } else if (st === 'booked' && lu[_bkNorm_(r['TICKET'])] === 'done') {
      // LOGISTICS UPDATE (AB) = Done -> badge Booked menjadi Delivered.
      r['Status'] = 'Delivered';
    }
    out.push(r);
  });
  out.sort(function (a, b) { return String(b['Timestamp']).localeCompare(String(a['Timestamp'])); });
  return { success: true, data: out };
}

// Batalkan booking: Status -> "Cancelled", catat waktu batal. Tiket & mesin otomatis
// bebas lagi (hanya Status "Booked" yang dihitung di _getBookedSets_), jadi mesin
// kembali dihitung RTD dan tiket bisa di-booking ulang.
function cancelMachineBooking_(data) {
  var bookingId = String(data.bookingId || '').trim();
  if (!bookingId) return { success: false, message: 'Booking ID tidak valid.' };

  var sheet = _getOrCreateMachineBookedSheet_();
  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return { success: false, message: 'Data booking tidak ditemukan.' };

  var colStatus = MACHINE_BOOKED_HEADERS.indexOf('Status') + 1;
  var colId = MACHINE_BOOKED_HEADERS.indexOf('Booking ID') + 1;
  var colTicket = MACHINE_BOOKED_HEADERS.indexOf('TICKET') + 1;
  var colCancelled = MACHINE_BOOKED_HEADERS.indexOf('Cancelled At') + 1;

  var ids = sheet.getRange(2, colId, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues();
  var rowNum = -1;
  for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
    if (String(ids[i][0]).trim() === bookingId) { rowNum = i + 2; break; }
  }
  if (rowNum === -1) return { success: false, message: 'Booking ' + bookingId + ' tidak ditemukan.' };

  var status = _bkNorm_(sheet.getRange(rowNum, colStatus).getValue());
  if (status === 'cancelled') return { success: true, message: 'Booking sudah berstatus Cancelled.', bookingId: bookingId };
  if (status !== 'booked') return { success: false, message: 'Hanya booking berstatus Booked yang bisa dibatalkan.' };

  var ticket = _bkNorm_(sheet.getRange(rowNum, colTicket).getValue());
  if (ticket && _bkGetLogisticsUpdateMap_()[ticket] === 'done') {
    return { success: false, message: 'Booking sudah Delivered (LOGISTICS UPDATE = Done), tidak bisa dibatalkan.' };
  }

  sheet.getRange(rowNum, colStatus).setValue('Cancelled');
  sheet.getRange(rowNum, colCancelled).setValue(new Date());
  SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  return { success: true, message: 'Booking dibatalkan.', bookingId: bookingId };
}

function submitMachineBooking_(data) {
  var bd = String(data.bd || '').trim();
  var actionPlan = String(data.actionPlan || '').trim();
  var ticket = String(data.ticket || '').trim();
  var machineKey = String(data.machineKey || '').trim();

  if (!bd || !actionPlan || !ticket || !machineKey) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Lengkapi BD, Action Plan, Location Name dan Type Machine.' };
  }
  if (BOOKING_ACTION_PLANS.indexOf(actionPlan) === -1) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Action Plan tidak valid: ' + actionPlan };
  }

  // Validasi ulang di server (data di browser bisa sudah basi / direbut BD lain).
  var bookedSets = _getBookedSets_();
  if (bookedSets.tickets[_bkNorm_(ticket)]) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Lokasi/tiket ' + ticket + ' sudah di-booking. Silakan refresh form.' };
  }
  var locs = _bkListOpenLocations_(bookedSets);
  if (locs.error) return { success: false, message: locs.error };
  var loc = null;
  locs.rows.forEach(function (l) { if (_bkNorm_(l.ticket) === _bkNorm_(ticket)) loc = l; });
  if (!loc) return { success: false, message: 'Tiket ' + ticket + ' sudah tidak tersedia (LOGISTICS UPDATE sudah terisi). Silakan refresh form.' };
  if (_bkNorm_(loc.bd) !== _bkNorm_(bd) || loc.actionPlan !== actionPlan) {
    return { success: false, message: 'BD / Action Plan tidak sesuai dengan data tiket ' + ticket + '.' };
  }

  var machine = null;
  _bkListRtdMachines_(bookedSets).forEach(function (m) { if (m.key === machineKey) machine = m; });
  if (!machine) return { success: false, message: 'Mesin sudah tidak berstatus RTD atau sudah di-booking BD lain. Silakan refresh form.' };

  var sheet = _getOrCreateMachineBookedSheet_();
  var bookingId = 'BK-' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyMMdd-HHmmss') + '-' +
    Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6).toUpperCase();
  function yn(v) { return v ? 'Ya' : 'Belum'; }

  var row = [
    new Date(), bd, actionPlan, loc.ticket, loc.locationName, loc.newVmPosition,
    machine.noVMC, machine.machineType,
    yn(data.akses), yn(data.pks), yn(data.permitLoading), yn(data.konfirmasiPicSite),
    'Booked', bookingId, machine.sheet, machine.serialNumber
  ];
  sheet.getRange(sheet.getLastRow() + 1, 1, 1, row.length).setValues([row]);
  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  return { success: true, message: 'Booking berhasil disimpan.', bookingId: bookingId };
}
